# Architecture Completion Audit

This audit maps the architecture roadmap to current source and validation evidence. The goal is not complete until every requirement is proven by source, tests, and build gates.

## Status

The core ownership, renderer, layout, text, platform, unsafe, dependency, performance, and validation boundaries have source, test, and build evidence. Dependency-version alignment is clear: `gpu-descriptor 0.3.2` is patched to upstream commit `79804e422186805f1ff5ab3d8310c07c145a6731`, the WGPU-stack `foldhash`/`hashbrown` split is gone, and `cargo xtask dependency-budget --strict` reports zero duplicate-version roots in normal and all-features workspace scopes. The desktop FPS budget has executable visible-X11 proof above 120 FPS for the perf-harness scenarios. Optimized web and Android release builds are on cache-backed validation paths after splitting the web wasm profile from native release LTO/codegen settings.

## Evidence Map

| Requirement | Status | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| One `AppContext` per `AppShell`; density, invalidation, node registry, focus, pointer, scroll, text, and shell FPS state are not process-global. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/render_state.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/lib.rs`, `crates/cranpose-testing/src/testing.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/lib.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/fps_monitor.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/tests/app_shell_tests.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/desktop.rs`, `crates/cranpose/tests/platform_scheduling_static.rs`. Production runtime access requires an active `AppContext`; only test and `test-helpers` builds expose an isolated per-thread context harness. Dropped contexts unregister from the thread-local id lookup table instead of leaving stale weak routing entries. Robot/perf diagnostics query the owning shell for FPS stats instead of reading a process-global publication slot. |
| Declarative native-window requests are owned by the desktop app instance. | Proven | `crates/cranpose/src/native_window.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/desktop.rs`, focused logs `native_window_registry_isolation_after.tmp`, `desktop_initial_native_registry_before.tmp`, `desktop_initial_native_registry_after.tmp`, and `native_window_after_initial_registry.tmp`, package log `native_window_registry_package_tests.tmp`, clippy log `native_window_registry_clippy.tmp`, and downstream X11 log `cranamp_x11_compare_after_initial_registry.tmp`. `NativeWindowRegistry` is stored on `App`; primary shell construction, native-window shell construction, primary updates, native-window updates, direct headless, and robot-driven update paths enter the registry scope. `native_window_requests_are_isolated_by_registry` proves two registries do not share request state, and the Cranamp exact-title comparison proves hidden-primary `run_windows` apps declare their visible OS windows during the initial stable render. |
| Linux native-window X11 probing is owned by the desktop app instance. | Proven | `crates/cranpose/src/desktop.rs`, `crates/cranpose/tests/platform_scheduling_static.rs`, failing log `desktop_x11_client_before.tmp`, passing logs `desktop_x11_client_after.tmp`, `desktop_x11_client_clippy.tmp`, and source scan `desktop_x11_client_source_scan.tmp`. `NativeWindowPlatformProbe` is stored on `App`, owns the cached X11 client, and all native-window pointer/position helpers receive the owning app probe explicitly instead of reading a process/thread-local `X11_WINDOW_CLIENT`. |
| Native-window dispatch keeps nested peer-window movement state scoped. | Proven | `crates/cranpose/src/native_window.rs`, failing log `native_window_dispatch_scope_before.tmp`, and passing log `native_window_dispatch_scope_after.tmp`. Dispatch now uses a stack-scoped `NativeWindowDispatchContext`, so nested native-window event handling restores the outer drag handler, resize handler, and surface origin instead of clearing the outer window's movement state. |
| Desktop performance stays above the production budget. | Guarded with native-release proof | `perf_robot_fps.sh`, `apps/desktop-demo/robot-runners/robot_perf_harness.rs`, `scripts/perf_robot_common.sh`, and `apps/desktop-demo/tests/source_hygiene_aliases.rs`. The visible-X11 FPS gate defaults to `CRANPOSE_PERF_MIN_FPS=120`, immediate present mode, cache-backed output paths, and all robot perf scenarios. Native-release evidence in `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/perf/fps_gate_after_concurrency_split.txt` passed under a hot host (`temp_c=max:95.8`) with `lazy_list_scroll=794.9 FPS`, `text_heavy_scroll=558.9 FPS`, `backdrop_blur=521.2 FPS`, and `opaque_scene=1428.8 FPS`; all sampled render summaries reported one submit per frame. |
| Winamp peer windows are movable in the visible desktop path. | Proven | `apps/desktop-demo/robot-runners/robot_winamp_native_window_geometry.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/native_window.rs`, failing log `native_window_dispatch_scope_before.tmp`, passing log `native_window_dispatch_scope_after.tmp`, and visible robot log `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/robot-winamp-native-window-geometry.out`. The framework guard proves nested native-window dispatch restores the outer drag/resize handlers; the visible robot proves real X11 geometry changes for group drag, one-pixel trace, fast drag, playlist/equalizer drags, and direct window-manager movement. |
| Text tab struck-through decoration renders as a solid line on the presented window. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/src/pipeline.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/pixels/src/pipeline.rs`, `apps/desktop-demo/robot-runners/robot_text_strikeout_presented.rs`, failing log `text_decoration_letter_spacing_before.tmp`, passing logs `text_decoration_segments_after_whitespace.tmp`, `text_decoration_pipeline_after_snap.tmp`, `pixels_text_decoration_after_snap.tmp`, `pixels_render_contract_after_snap.tmp`, and `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/robot-text-strikeout-presented.out`. WGPU draws decorations over foreground glyphs, merges decorated runs across letter-spacing and whitespace gaps, and snaps decoration bands to integral rows. Pixels decorations request final draw-item pixel snapping after the translated-content snap anchor, preserving the shared rigid-translation render contract. The presented X11 crop at `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/robot-output/cranpose_text_strikeout_presented/strikeout_crop.png` was inspected after the fix and shows a continuous solid strikeout band; the robot now passes. |
| Android host-window requests are owned by the Android runtime instance. | Proven | `crates/cranpose/src/android_host_window.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/android.rs`, failing log `android_host_window_registry_before.tmp`, passing logs `android_host_window_registry_after.tmp`, `android_host_window_registry_tests.tmp`, `android_host_window_registry_package_tests.tmp`, and clippy log `android_host_window_registry_clippy.tmp`. `AndroidHostWindowRegistry` is stored in the Android runtime loop, `shell.update()` enters the registry scope, geometry synchronization and dispatch read the explicit registry, and `host_window_requests_are_isolated_by_registry` proves two registries do not share request state. |
| Android overlay events are owned by the Android runtime instance. | Proven | `crates/cranpose/src/android_overlay_window.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/android_jni.rs`, `crates/cranpose/android/java/dev/cranpose/android/CranposeOverlayWindow.java`, `crates/cranpose/src/android.rs`, failing static logs `android_overlay_queue_static_before.tmp` and `android_overlay_handle_before.tmp`, passing logs `android_overlay_queue_static_after.tmp`, `android_overlay_handle_after.tmp`, `android_overlay_handle_after_fmt.tmp`, and Android build log `apps/android-demo/android/android-overlay-handle-gradle.tmp`. `AndroidOverlayEventQueue` is created by the Android runtime loop, Java callbacks carry an explicit retained runtime queue handle, Rust keeps no process-global overlay queue or helper-class cache, and overlay event draining requires the explicit runtime queue. |
| Layout cache epochs are owned by the active app context. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/render_state.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/layout/mod.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/layout/tests/layout_tests.rs`, failing log `ui_layout_epoch_context_before.tmp`, passing logs `ui_layout_epoch_context_after.tmp`, `ui_layout_epoch_context_tests.tmp`, and clippy log `ui_layout_epoch_context_clippy.tmp`. `AppContext` owns the layout cache epoch counter, layout invalidation and dirty measurement advance the active context epoch, and `cache_epoch_is_isolated_by_app_context` proves one context's dirty measures do not advance another context's cache generation. |
| Modifier-chain trace subscribers are owned by the active app context. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/render_state.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/debug.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/modifier/tests/modifier_tests.rs`, failing log `ui_modifier_trace_context_before.tmp`, passing logs `ui_modifier_trace_context_after.tmp`, `ui_modifier_trace_context_tests.tmp`, and clippy log `ui_modifier_trace_context_clippy.tmp`. Trace installation requires an active `AppContext`, trace emission reads the current context-local callback, and `modifier_chain_trace_is_app_context_owned` proves two contexts do not overwrite each other's subscribers. |
| Fling velocity diagnostics are owned by the active app context. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/render_state.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/modifier/scroll.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/desktop.rs`, failing log `fling_velocity_context_before.tmp`, passing logs `fling_velocity_context_after.tmp`, `ui_fling_velocity_context_tests.tmp`, `platform_static_after_fling_velocity_context.tmp`, `fling_velocity_robot_examples_check.tmp`, and `fling_velocity_cranpose_clippy.tmp`. Fling detection records diagnostic velocity into the current `AppContext`, robot tests query/reset it through app-thread robot commands, and `app_contexts_keep_fling_velocity_diagnostics_isolated` proves two contexts do not share the diagnostic value. |
| Scroll ids and invalidation callback ids are instance-owned. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/scroll.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/tests/scroll_tests.rs`, failing logs `ui_scroll_callback_ids_before.tmp` and `ui_scroll_state_id_source_before.tmp`, passing logs `ui_scroll_callback_ids_after.tmp`, `ui_scroll_callback_id_instance_tests.tmp`, `ui_scroll_callback_scroll_tests.tmp`, `ui_scroll_callback_ids_clippy.tmp`, `ui_scroll_state_id_source_after.tmp`, `ui_scroll_state_id_instance_after.tmp`, `ui_scroll_state_id_clippy.tmp`, and source scan `ui_scroll_state_id_source_scan.tmp`. `ScrollState::id()` is derived from retained inner object identity, `ScrollStateInner` and `ScrollMotionContextInner` own their callback id counters, and tests prove clones retain ids while separate instances do not share id or callback allocators. |
| Web frame wakeups are owned by the web AppShell instance. | Proven | `crates/cranpose/src/web.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/lib.rs`, failing log `web_frame_waker_router_before.tmp`, passing log `web_frame_waker_router_after.tmp`, clippy log `web_frame_waker_router_clippy.tmp`, source scan `web_frame_waker_router_source_scan.tmp`, and wasm build log `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/desktop-demo-build-web-after-web-frame-waker.out`. The wasm AppShell frame waker is single-threaded, the web runtime installs its per-shell requester directly, and `WEB_FRAME_REQUESTER`/`install_web_frame_requester`/`request_current_web_frame` are gone. |
| AppContext invalidation queues survive mutex poisoning. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/render_state.rs`, focused log `render_state_repass_tests.tmp`. Layout, draw, and modifier-slice repass queues recover their inner state from poisoned mutexes instead of turning future invalidations into permanent panics. |
| Shared debug/platform queues recover from lock poisoning. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/debug.rs`, `crates/cranpose/src/android_overlay_window.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/lib.rs`, `crates/cranpose-runtime-std/src/lib.rs`, focused logs `debug_trace_tests.tmp`, `android_overlay_window_tests.tmp`, `app_shell_frame_scheduler_poison_test.tmp`, `runtime_std_scheduler_poison_tests.tmp`, and `runtime_std_scheduler_poison_clippy.tmp`. Debug modifier-chain tracing, Android overlay event queues, AppShell frame-deadline scheduling, and native `StdScheduler` frame-waker storage recover their inner state from poisoned locks. Native frame-waker replacement and clearing drop the old waker outside the lock, so a panic-on-drop callback cannot permanently break future frame scheduling. |
| Event dispatch and runtime wakeups enter the owning app context. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/shell_input.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/hit_path_tracker.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/common/src/hit_graph.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/modifier/pointer_input.rs`, `crates/cranpose-app-shell/src/tests/app_shell_tests.rs`, `crates/cranpose-testing/tests/pointer_input_*`. Hit-path dispatch tree construction uses guarded map entries instead of a parent-ordering panic assumption; hit-graph pointer-input ancestry carries the already-checked node id instead of re-reading it through an `expect` path. |
| Pointer-input ids are retained-object-owned. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/modifier/pointer_input.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/tests/modifier_nodes_tests.rs`, failing log `ui_pointer_input_ids_before.tmp`, passing logs `ui_pointer_input_ids_after.tmp`, `ui_pointer_input_ids_modifier_tests.tmp`, `ui_pointer_input_ids_clippy.tmp`, and source scan `ui_pointer_input_ids_source_scan.tmp`. | Core UI continuation dispatch survives malformed cross-thread payloads and dropped runtime channels. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-core/src/runtime.rs`, focused logs `core_ui_continuation_tests.tmp`, `core_ui_continuation_clippy.tmp`, `core_ui_dispatcher_pending_tests.tmp`, `core_ui_dispatcher_pending_clippy.tmp`, `core_pending_guard_tests.tmp`, and `core_pending_guard_clippy.tmp`. | Layout runtime state is retained by node identity. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-ui/src/layout/mod.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/widgets/nodes/layout_node.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/widgets/lazy_list.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/tests/modifier_nodes_tests.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/layout/tests/layout_tests.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/layout/tests/policies_tests.rs`, `crates/cranpose-foundation/src/lazy/diagnostics.rs`, `crates/cranpose-foundation/src/lazy/bounds_adjuster.rs`, `crates/cranpose-foundation/src/modifier.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/modifier/mod.rs`, `crates/cranpose-ui/src/modifier/local.rs`. Child measurable wrapper and state pointers are retained across clean measure passes and reconciled by child id when child order changes; per-frame child measure bindings are cleared after node measurement so the AppContext-owned frame arena returns after retained child measurement. `VecPools` stores acquired scratch vectors directly and returns them with `mem::take` on drop, so the layout hot path does not depend on one-shot `Option` postconditions. `LayoutMeasurements` exposes the optional debug layout tree explicitly when measurement skips tree allocation. Modifier concatenation extracts optional single-part slices after empty short-circuiting, so the public `Modifier::then()` path is total instead of guarded by enum-state `unreachable!` branches. Retained `ModifierNodeChain` entries are now matched by element type and node type, and each candidate must prove it can update the stored node before reuse; mismatched retained nodes are replaced and detached instead of flowing into a downcast-panic update path. Modifier-local reads fall back to the requested key default on provider/ancestor type mismatch and reconcile erased fallback-cache entries back to the requested key type, so modifier-local dependency tracking no longer depends on cache postcondition panics. The unused `MeasurementProxy` layer is removed, custom/stateful layout modifier tests prove retained coordinators invoke live nodes instead of detached measurement snapshots, and lazy-list diagnostic env/counter state is centralized in one private diagnostics module instead of repeated process statics across hot layout modules. Lazy-list interval misses become empty measured items or skipped prefetch work instead of panicking inside measurement, and lazy bounds adjustment treats empty item slices as a no-op without relying on a post-empty-check unwrap. |

| WGPU effects, shadows, offscreen surfaces, projective composites, readback, texture uploads, buffer uploads, and transient textures record into executor-owned frame command paths. | Proven | `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/src/frame_graph.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/src/effect_renderer.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/src/offscreen.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/src/render.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/src/surface_executor`, `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/tests/render_contract.rs`, `crates/cranpose-render/wgpu/tests/effect_semantics.rs`. Native root rendering now creates a `WgpuFrameGraph` recorded frame pass, and `PassContext` implements `FrameCommandRecorder`, so the main frame no longer goes through a separate native command-recorder submit shell. Unsupported-effect warning suppression is renderer-owned, and source contracts reject a process-global WGPU warning static. Texture uploads enter through `WgpuFrameGraphExecutor::upload_texture`; `texture_uploads_are_owned_by_frame_graph_executor` rejects `write_texture` outside `frame_graph.rs`. Buffer uploads enter through `WgpuFrameGraphExecutor::upload_buffer`; `buffer_uploads_are_owned_by_frame_graph_executor` rejects `write_buffer` outside `frame_graph.rs`. Effect/shader/blur/composite encoders receive upload operations through `FrameCommandRecorder`; renderer contracts reject raw `wgpu::Queue`, raw `FrameUploadAllocators`, and recorder `command_parts()` access in the effect boundary. Render-effect scratch acquisition and consumption mismatches return through the renderer error path instead of panicking inside effect encoding. Recorded-graph declaration and scheduling failures return `FrameGraphError`, and screenshot readback maps that through the renderer error path after restoring the retained executor. Recorded pass encode failures return `FrameGraphError::PassFailed`; the executor releases pending transient textures and returns before the submit point, so native rendering cannot submit a partial failed frame through an out-of-band result slot. Offscreen texture bind groups and uniform upload bind groups are cached with `OnceCell`, so effect passes borrow cached bind groups directly instead of relying on RefCell/Option postconditions. The WASM frame encoder consumes its command encoder on `finish(self)` and uses a transient-release owner for cleanup, so submitted encoder access is no longer guarded by a panic-only `Option` postcondition. |

Incremental scene update hit rebuilding falls back to a full graph rebuild if the retained graph is absent. Segment draw batch type mismatches return renderer errors and shadow cursor misses are represented as `None`, instead of aborting with `unreachable!` in the command encoder path. Effect-layer destination mismatches release frame surfaces and return renderer errors instead of aborting after allocating an offscreen source. |

## Validation Snapshot

Current repo-local validation evidence after the target-specific WGPU backend split, unsafe-boundary guard, no-tmpfs build-script guard, dependency-budget tightening, real-window X11 comparison pass, renderer scale-sentinel robot guard, and explicit visible-X11 robot runner guard:

- Text/Winamp regression refresh: `native_window_dispatch_scope_before.tmp` first failed on nested dispatch clearing the outer native-window drag/resize scope, then `native_window_dispatch_scope_after.tmp` passed after dispatch used a stack-scoped `NativeWindowDispatchContext`. `text_decoration_letter_spacing_before.tmp` first failed on split decorated spans, then `text_decoration_segments_after_whitespace.tmp`, `text_decoration_pipeline_after_snap.tmp`, `pixels_text_decoration_after_snap.tmp`, and `pixels_render_contract_after_snap.tmp` passed after decoration merging, draw order, and final pixel snapping were corrected. Visible X11 robots `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/robot-text-strikeout-presented.out` and `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/robot-winamp-native-window-geometry.out` passed; the Text tab crop was inspected at `/home/s/.cache/cranpose/robot-output/cranpose_text_strikeout_presented/strikeout_crop.png`.
- Hidden-primary `run_windows` refresh: `desktop_initial_native_registry_before.tmp` first failed because desktop `AppShell::new_with_size_and_density` ran its initial stable render before the app-owned `NativeWindowRegistry` scope existed. `desktop_initial_native_registry_after.tmp` and `native_window_after_initial_registry.tmp` passed after both primary and native-window shell construction entered that registry scope. The exact-title downstream Cranamp comparison `cranamp_x11_compare_after_window_wait.tmp` first failed because the current build launched but never produced `Cranamp Winamp`; after the construction-scope fix, `cranamp_x11_compare_after_initial_registry.tmp` passed with baseline/current windows owned by `cranamp`, both captured at `372x157`, identical content bounds `372x157+0+0`, and normalized RMSE `0.0268588`.
- Post-initial-native-registry validation refresh: `bash -n scripts/x11_compare_window.sh scripts/x11_compare_downstream_cranpose.sh scripts/x11_compare_origin_main.sh perf_robot_fps.sh scripts/perf_robot_common.sh`, `x11_window_wait_hygiene_after_initial_registry.tmp`, `desktop_initial_native_registry_after_docs.tmp`, `native_window_after_initial_registry_docs.tmp`, `desktop_initial_native_registry_clippy.tmp`, `post_initial_registry_fmt_check.tmp`, `post_initial_registry_diff_check.tmp`, `post_initial_registry_docs_fmt_check.tmp`, `post_initial_registry_docs_diff_check.tmp`, `robot_app_cfg_scan_after_initial_registry.tmp`, `post_initial_registry_log_scan.tmp`, `cargo test -j 2 > 1.tmp 2>&1`, `cargo clippy -j 2 --all-targets -- -D warnings > 2.tmp 2>&1`, `no_default_build_after_initial_registry.tmp`, `all_features_check_after_initial_registry.tmp`, and `dependency_budget_after_initial_registry.tmp` passed with cache-backed temp paths. The dependency budget still reports only the tracked WGPU-stack `foldhash` and `hashbrown` split.

## Completion Gate

The final dependency and cross-target validation gates are closed. Future work
should treat any duplicate-version family, multi-submit renderer regression,
presented-window scale drift, Text tab decoration artifact, Winamp native-window
movement failure, or sub-120-FPS scenario as a release blocker.
